```cpp
// Mostra una sezione: i controlli delle altre pagine si nascondono (e escono dall'ordine del Tab).
void ShowPage(int p) {
  if (p < 0 || p >= kPageCount) return;
  g.page = p;
  const HWND focus = GetFocus();
  for (size_t i = 0; i < g.layout.size(); ++i) {
    const int pg = g.layoutPage[i];
    if (pg < 0) continue;
    const HWND c = g.layout[i].first;
    if (pg != p && (c == focus || IsChild(c, focus))) SetFocus(Item(IDC_NAV_BASE + p));
    ShowWindow(c, pg == p ? SW_SHOWNA : SW_HIDE);
  }
  for (int i = 0; i < kPageCount; ++i) InvalidateRect(Item(IDC_NAV_BASE + i), nullptr, FALSE);
  InvalidateRect(g.wnd, nullptr, FALSE);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ disegno
void DrawTextAt(HDC dc, const std::wstring& t, RECT r, HFONT f, COLORREF c, UINT fmt) {
  SelectObject(dc, f);
  SetTextColor(dc, c);
  DrawTextW(dc, t.c_str(), int(t.size()), &r, fmt | DT_NOPREFIX | DT_SINGLELINE);
}

std::wstring StatusPillText() { return g.monitorRunning ? T(L"Overlay in esecuzione") : T(L"Overlay non avviato"); }

// Badge dello stato in fondo alla barra laterale.
RECT StatusPillRect() { return SR({16, 596, 208, 626}); }

// Il pulsante "Aggiorna a vX" sta in fondo alla barra laterale; compare solo se c'è una versione nuova.
void PlaceUpdateButton() {
  HWND b = Item(IDC_UPDATE);
  if (!b) return;
  if (!g.update) {
    ShowWindow(b, SW_HIDE);
    return;
  }
  SetWindowTextW(b, TF(L"Aggiorna a v{}", ToWide(g.update->version)).c_str());
  ShowWindow(b, SW_SHOW);
}

// Controllo in background all'apertura: se c'è una versione nuova compare il pulsante.
void StartUpdateCheck() {
  if (!g.cfg.checkUpdates) return;
  std::thread([wnd = g.wnd] {
    std::wstring error;
    auto u = CheckForUpdate(error);
    if (!u) return;
    auto* info = new UpdateInfo(std::move(*u));
    if (!PostMessageW(wnd, kMsgUpdateFound, 0, reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(info))) delete info;
  }).detach();
}

void OnUpdateClicked() {
  if (!g.update) return;
  const UpdateInfo& u = *g.update;
  std::wstring msg = TF(L"È disponibile PerfOverlay Supreme v{} (in uso: v{}).", ToWide(u.version), ToWide(AppVersion()));
  if (!u.notes.empty()) msg += L"\n\n" + ToWide(u.notes);
  msg += T(L"\n\nScaricarla e installarla ora? Impostazioni e overlay si riavviano da soli.");
  if (MessageBoxW(g.wnd, msg.c_str(), kAppName, MB_YESNO | MB_ICONINFORMATION) != IDYES) return;
  if (UiToProfile() && UiToGlobal()) SaveAll();  // le modifiche in corso non vanno perse
  EnableWindow(Item(IDC_UPDATE), FALSE);
  Status(TF(L"Scaricamento della versione v{}...", ToWide(u.version)), ui::col::Accent);
  std::thread([wnd = g.wnd, u] {
    std::wstring error;
    const bool ok = DownloadUpdate(u, error);
    auto* err = new std::wstring(error);
    if (!PostMessageW(wnd, kMsgUpdateDownloaded, ok, reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(err))) delete err;
  }).detach();
}

// Exe scaricato e verificato: chiude l'overlay, sostituisce l'exe e riapre tutto con la nuova versione.
void OnUpdateDownloaded(bool ok, std::wstring error) {
  if (ok) {
    HWND monitor = FindWindowW(kMonitorWndClass, nullptr);
    const bool monitorWasRunning = monitor != nullptr;
    if (monitor) {
      PostMessageW(monitor, kMsgQuit, 0, 0);
      for (int i = 0; i < 40 && FindWindowW(kMonitorWndClass, nullptr); ++i) Sleep(125);
    }
    std::wstring installError;
    if (InstallUpdate(installError)) {
      const auto exe = ExePath();
      ShellExecuteW(nullptr, L"open", exe.c_str(), L"--settings", nullptr, SW_SHOWNORMAL);
      if (monitorWasRunning) ShellExecuteW(nullptr, L"open", exe.c_str(), nullptr, nullptr, SW_SHOWNORMAL);
      DestroyWindow(g.wnd);
      return;
    }
    if (monitorWasRunning) ShellExecuteW(nullptr, L"open", ExePath().c_str(), nullptr, nullptr, SW_SHOWNORMAL);
    ok = false;
    error = installError;
  }
  EnableWindow(Item(IDC_UPDATE), TRUE);
  Status(T(L"Aggiornamento non riuscito: ") + error, ui::col::Bad);
  if (g.update && !g.update->pageUrl.empty() &&
      MessageBoxW(g.wnd, (error + T(L"\n\nAprire la pagina della release?")).c_str(), kAppName,
                  MB_YESNO | MB_ICONWARNING) == IDYES)
    ShellExecuteW(nullptr, L"open", ToWide(g.update->pageUrl).c_str(), nullptr, nullptr, SW_SHOWNORMAL);
}

void Paint(HDC dc, const RECT& client) {
  FillRect(dc, &client, g.brBg);
  // Barra laterale a tutta altezza, con una riga di separazione.
  const RECT side{0, 0, g.ox + S(kSideW), client.bottom};
  FillRect(dc, &side, g.brSide);
  HBRUSH line = CreateSolidBrush(ui::col::Line);
  const RECT sep{side.right - 1, 0, side.right, client.bottom};
  FillRect(dc, &sep, line);
  DeleteObject(line);
  SetBkMode(dc, TRANSPARENT);
  SetViewportOrgEx(dc, g.ox, g.oy, nullptr);  // contenuto centrato nella finestra

  // Marchio: icona, nome con "Supreme" nel colore d'accento, versione.
  DrawIconEx(dc, S(18), S(20), g.icon, S(32), S(32), 0, nullptr, DI_NORMAL);
  SelectObject(dc, g.fontSection);
  SIZE nw{};
  GetTextExtentPoint32W(dc, L"PerfOverlay ", 12, &nw);
  DrawTextAt(dc, L"PerfOverlay ", SR({60, 16, 220, 38}), g.fontSection, ui::col::Text, DT_LEFT | DT_BOTTOM);
  DrawTextAt(dc, L"Supreme", {S(60) + nw.cx, S(16), S(220), S(38)}, g.fontSection, ui::col::Accent, DT_LEFT | DT_BOTTOM);
  DrawTextAt(dc, L"v" + ToWide(AppVersion()), SR({60, 38, 220, 56}), g.font, ui::col::Sub, DT_LEFT | DT_TOP);

  // Stato dell'overlay: badge arrotondato con pallino, verde se in esecuzione.
  const std::wstring state = StatusPillText();
  const COLORREF stateColor = g.monitorRunning ? ui::col::Good : ui::col::Bad;
  const RECT pill = StatusPillRect();
  ui::FillRound(dc, pill, S(15), ui::Blend(kSidebar, stateColor, 0.12f), ui::Blend(kSidebar, stateColor, 0.45f));
  ui::FillCircle(dc, pill.left + S(17), (pill.top + pill.bottom) / 2, S(4), stateColor, stateColor);
  DrawTextAt(dc, state, {pill.left + S(29), pill.top, pill.right - S(10), pill.bottom}, g.fontBold, stateColor,
             DT_LEFT | DT_VCENTER | DT_END_ELLIPSIS);

  // Intestazione della sezione.
  const PageInfo& pg = kPages[g.page];
  DrawTextAt(dc, pg.name, SR({248, 18, 976, 52}), g.fontTitle, ui::col::Text, DT_LEFT | DT_VCENTER);
  std::wstring sub = pg.subtitle ? pg.subtitle : L"";
  if (!pg.subtitle && g.cur >= 0 && size_t(g.cur) < g.profiles.size())
    sub = TF(L"Valgono per il profilo \"{}\": lo scegli nella sezione Profili.", ToWide(g.profiles[size_t(g.cur)].name));
  DrawTextAt(dc, sub, SR({249, 54, 976, 76}), g.font, ui::col::Sub, DT_LEFT | DT_VCENTER | DT_END_ELLIPSIS);

  for (const auto& p : g_panels) {
    if (p.page != g.page) continue;
    const RECT r = SR(p.r);
    DrawTextAt(dc, p.title, {r.left + S(4), r.top - S(24), r.right, r.top - S(4)}, g.fontSection, ui::col::Text,
               DT_LEFT | DT_BOTTOM);
    ui::FillRound(dc, r, S(12), ui::col::Panel, ui::col::Line);
  }
  for (const auto& l : g_labels)
    if (l.page == g.page) DrawTextAt(dc, l.text, SR(l.r), g.font, ui::col::Sub, DT_LEFT | DT_VCENTER);
  for (const auto& f : g_frames) {
    if (f.page != g.page) continue;
    const bool on = IsWindowEnabled(Item(f.id));
    const bool active = on && (g.focusedEdit == f.id || g_capturing == f.id);
    ui::FillRound(dc, SR(f.r), S(8), on ? ui::col::Input : kInputOff,
                  !on ? RGB(44, 44, 52) : active ? ui::col::Accent : ui::col::Field, active ? float(S(2)) : 1.0f);
  }
  DrawTextAt(dc, g.status, StatusRect(), g.font, g.statusColor, DT_LEFT | DT_VCENTER | DT_END_ELLIPSIS);
  SetViewportOrgEx(dc, 0, 0, nullptr);  // la copia del buffer sullo schermo usa coordinate della finestra
}

// Voce della barra laterale: icona e nome; la sezione aperta ha fondo chiaro e barretta d'accento.
void DrawNavItem(const DRAWITEMSTRUCT* d, int i) {
  HDC dc = d->hDC;
  const RECT& r = d->rcItem;
  FillRect(dc, &r, g.brSide);
  const bool sel = i == g.page;
  const bool hover = ui::IsHovered(d->hwndItem);
  const bool focus = (d->itemState & ODS_FOCUS) && !(d->itemState & ODS_NOFOCUSRECT);
  if (sel || hover) {
    const COLORREF fill = sel ? RGB(38, 38, 46) : RGB(30, 30, 36);
    ui::FillRound(dc, r, S(8), fill, fill, 0);
  }
  if (sel) ui::FillRound(dc, {r.left, r.top + S(9), r.left + S(3), r.bottom - S(9)}, S(2), ui::col::Accent, ui::col::Accent, 0);
  if (focus) ui::StrokeRound(dc, r, S(8), ui::col::Accent, float(S(2)));
  SetBkMode(dc, TRANSPARENT);
  DrawTextAt(dc, kPages[i].glyph, {r.left + S(14), r.top, r.left + S(40), r.bottom}, g.fontIcon,
             sel ? ui::col::Accent : ui::col::Sub, DT_LEFT | DT_VCENTER);
  DrawTextAt(dc, kPages[i].name, {r.left + S(46), r.top, r.right - S(8), r.bottom}, sel ? g.fontBold : g.font,
             sel ? ui::col::Text : RGB(200, 200, 210), DT_LEFT | DT_VCENTER | DT_END_ELLIPSIS);
}

void DrawProfileItem(const DRAWITEMSTRUCT* d) {
  if (d->itemID == UINT(-1)) return;
  HDC dc = d->hDC;
  const RECT& r = d->rcItem;
  FillRect(dc, &r, g.brPanel);
  const bool sel = d->itemState & ODS_SELECTED;
  if (sel) {
    RECT b = r;
    InflateRect(&b, -S(2), -S(2));
    ui::FillRound(dc, b, S(6), ui::Blend(ui::col::Panel, ui::col::Accent, 0.20f),
                  ui::Blend(ui::col::Panel, ui::col::Accent, 0.55f));
  }
  wchar_t buf[256];
  SendMessageW(d->hwndItem, LB_GETTEXT, d->itemID, reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(buf));
  const std::wstring text = buf;
  const auto tab = text.find(L'\t');
  SetBkMode(dc, TRANSPARENT);
  RECT tr{r.left + S(12), r.top, r.right - S(12), r.bottom};
  if (tab != std::wstring::npos)
    DrawTextAt(dc, text.substr(tab + 1), tr, g.font, sel ? ui::col::AccentHover : ui::col::Sub,
               DT_RIGHT | DT_VCENTER);
  tr.right -= S(78);
  DrawTextAt(dc, text.substr(0, tab), tr, sel ? g.fontBold : g.font, ui::col::Text,
             DT_LEFT | DT_VCENTER | DT_END_ELLIPSIS);
}

void UpdateMonitorState() {
  const bool running = FindWindowW(kMonitorWndClass, nullptr) != nullptr;
  if (running == g.monitorRunning && IsWindowVisible(Item(IDC_LAUNCH)) == !running) return;
  g.monitorRunning = running;
  ShowWindow(Item(IDC_LAUNCH), running ? SW_HIDE : SW_SHOW);
  PlaceUpdateButton();
  const RECT r = ToClient(SR({0, 490, kSideW, kClientH}));
  InvalidateRect(g.wnd, &r, FALSE);
}

void Relayout(int clientW, int clientH);

LRESULT CALLBACK WndProc(HWND hwnd, UINT msg, WPARAM wp, LPARAM lp) {
  switch (msg) {
    case WM_SIZE:
      if (wp != SIZE_MINIMIZED && g.wnd) Relayout(LOWORD(lp), HIWORD(lp));
      return 0;
    case WM_GETMINMAXINFO: {  // non più piccola del 60% (il testo resta leggibile)
      RECT r{0, 0, MulDiv(kClientW, g.baseDpi * 6, 960), MulDiv(kClientH, g.baseDpi * 6, 960)};
      AdjustWindowRectExForDpi(&r, DWORD(GetWindowLongW(hwnd, GWL_STYLE)), FALSE, 0, UINT(g.baseDpi));
      auto* mm = reinterpret_cast<MINMAXINFO*>(lp);
      mm->ptMinTrackSize = {r.right - r.left, r.bottom - r.top};
      return 0;
    }
    case WM_DPICHANGED: {  // finestra spostata su un monitor con DPI diversi
      g.baseDpi = HIWORD(wp);
      const auto* r = reinterpret_cast<const RECT*>(lp);
      SetWindowPos(hwnd, nullptr, r->left, r->top, r->right - r->left, r->bottom - r->top,
                   SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE);
      return 0;
    }
    case WM_PAINT: {
      PAINTSTRUCT ps;
      HDC dc = BeginPaint(hwnd, &ps);
      RECT rc;
      GetClientRect(hwnd, &rc);
      HDC mem = CreateCompatibleDC(dc);
      HBITMAP bmp = CreateCompatibleBitmap(dc, rc.right, rc.bottom);
      HGDIOBJ old = SelectObject(mem, bmp);
      Paint(mem, rc);
      BitBlt(dc, ps.rcPaint.left, ps.rcPaint.top, ps.rcPaint.right - ps.rcPaint.left,
             ps.rcPaint.bottom - ps.rcPaint.top, mem, ps.rcPaint.left, ps.rcPaint.top, SRCCOPY);
      SelectObject(mem, old);
      DeleteObject(bmp);
      DeleteDC(mem);
      EndPaint(hwnd, &ps);
      return 0;
    }
    case WM_ERASEBKGND:
      return 1;
    case WM_TIMER:
      if (wp == kTimerMonitor) UpdateMonitorState();
      return 0;
    case WM_MEASUREITEM: {
      auto* m = reinterpret_cast<MEASUREITEMSTRUCT*>(lp);
      if (m->CtlType == ODT_LISTBOX) m->itemHeight = UINT(S(32));
      return TRUE;
    }
    case WM_DRAWITEM: {
      auto* d = reinterpret_cast<const DRAWITEMSTRUCT*>(lp);
      if (d->CtlType == ODT_LISTBOX) {
        DrawProfileItem(d);
      } else if (d->CtlType == ODT_BUTTON) {
        const int ci = int(d->CtlID) - IDC_COLOR_BTN;
        const int ai = int(d->CtlID) - IDC_ACCENT_BASE;
        const int ni = int(d->CtlID) - IDC_NAV_BASE;
        if (ni >= 0 && ni < kPageCount) {
          DrawNavItem(d, ni);
        } else if (ai >= 0 && ai < kAccentCount) {
          DrawAccentChip(d, kAccentPresets[ai].color);
        } else if (ci >= 0 && ci < kColorCount && g.cur >= 0) {
          const Color c = ColorRef(g.profiles[size_t(g.cur)], ci);
          const COLORREF sw = RGB(c.r, c.g, c.b);
          ui::DrawButton(d, &sw);
        } else {
          ui::DrawButton(d);
        }
      }
      return TRUE;
    }
    case WM_CTLCOLOREDIT: {
      HDC dc = reinterpret_cast<HDC>(wp);
      SetTextColor(dc, ui::col::Text);
      SetBkColor(dc, ui::col::Input);
      return reinterpret_cast<LRESULT>(g.brInput);
    }
    case WM_CTLCOLORSTATIC: {  // EDIT disattivati o in sola lettura (hotkey)
      HDC dc = reinterpret_cast<HDC>(wp);
      const HWND ctl = reinterpret_cast<HWND>(lp);
      if (IsWindowEnabled(ctl)) {
        SetTextColor(dc, g_capturing == GetDlgCtrlID(ctl) ? ui::col::AccentHover : ui::col::Text);
        SetBkColor(dc, ui::col::Input);
        return reinterpret_cast<LRESULT>(g.brInput);
      }
      SetTextColor(dc, ui::col::Disabled);
      SetBkColor(dc, kInputOff);
      return reinterpret_cast<LRESULT>(g.brInputOff);
    }
    case WM_CTLCOLORLISTBOX: {
      HDC dc = reinterpret_cast<HDC>(wp);
      SetTextColor(dc, ui::col::Text);
      if (reinterpret_cast<HWND>(lp) == Item(IDC_LIST)) {
        SetBkColor(dc, ui::col::Panel);
        return reinterpret_cast<LRESULT>(g.brPanel);
      }
      SetBkColor(dc, ui::col::Input);  // tendina delle combobox
      return reinterpret_cast<LRESULT>(g.brInput);
    }
    case WM_COMMAND: {
      const int id = LOWORD(wp), code = HIWORD(wp);
      const bool isEdit = std::any_of(g_frames.begin(), g_frames.end(), [id](const FrameItem& f) { return f.id == id; });
      if (isEdit && (code == EN_SETFOCUS || code == EN_KILLFOCUS)) {
        g.focusedEdit = code == EN_SETFOCUS ? id : 0;
        for (const auto& f : g_frames)
          if (f.id == id) {
            RECT r = ToClient(SR(f.r));
            InvalidateRect(hwnd, &r, FALSE);
          }
        return 0;
      }
      if (id == IDC_ACCENT_WHEEL && code == ui::kColorWheelChanged) {
        ApplyAccent(COLORREF(SendMessageW(Item(IDC_ACCENT_WHEEL), ui::kMsgWheelGetColor, 0, 0)), IDC_ACCENT_WHEEL);
        return 0;
      }
      if (id == IDC_ACCENT_HEX && code == EN_CHANGE) {
        COLORREF c = 0;
        if (!g.syncingAccent && ui::ParseHexColor(GetUtf8(IDC_ACCENT_HEX), c)) ApplyAccent(c, IDC_ACCENT_HEX);
        return 0;
      }
      if (id == IDC_LIST && code == LBN_SELCHANGE) {
        OnSelectProfile();
      } else if (id == IDC_LANG && code == CBN_SELCHANGE) {
        OnChangeLanguage();
      } else if (id == IDC_POSITION && code == CBN_SELCHANGE) {
        const bool custom = GetSel(IDC_POSITION) == 4;
        EnableField(IDC_X, custom);
        EnableField(IDC_Y, custom);
      } else if (code == BN_CLICKED && id >= IDC_HK_BTN && id < IDC_HK_BTN + 3) {
        const int edit = kHotkeyIds[id - IDC_HK_BTN];
        if (g_capturing == edit) {
          EndCapture(false);
          Status(T(L"Modifica hotkey annullata."));
        } else {
          StartCapture(edit);
        }
      } else if (code == BN_CLICKED) {
        if (id >= IDC_COLOR_BTN && id < IDC_COLOR_BTN + kColorCount) OnPickColor(id - IDC_COLOR_BTN);
        if (id >= IDC_ACCENT_BASE && id < IDC_ACCENT_BASE + kAccentCount) ApplyAccent(kAccentPresets[id - IDC_ACCENT_BASE].color);
        if (id == IDC_ACCENT_RESET) ApplyAccent(ui::col::DefaultAccent);
        if (id >= IDC_NAV_BASE && id < IDC_NAV_BASE + kPageCount) {
          EndCapture(false);
          ShowPage(id - IDC_NAV_BASE);
        }
        switch (id) {
          case IDC_IMPORT_RTSS: OnImportRtss(); break;
          case IDC_UPDATE: OnUpdateClicked(); break;
          case IDC_EDITOR:
            if (UiToProfile() && UiToGlobal()) {
              Profile& p = g.profiles[size_t(g.cur)];
              if (p.layout == "rtss" && p.rtss.advanced) {
                MessageBoxW(g.wnd,
                            T(L"Questo è un preset RTSS avanzato (immagini, grafici e formule, es. TroyMetrics): "
                            L"viene mostrato così com'è stato disegnato nell'OverlayEditor di RTSS.\n\n"
                            L"Qui puoi cambiarne posizione (Posizione, X / Y) e grandezza (Dimensione)."),
                            kAppName, MB_ICONINFORMATION);
                break;
              }
              if (RunLayoutEditor(g.wnd, g.inst, g.fontDlg, p)) {  // imposta da solo il layout (libero o RTSS)
                ProfileToUi();
                OfferUseEverywhere(p);
                SaveAll();
                Status(TF(L"{} di \"{}\" salvato e applicato.",
                                   p.layout == "rtss" ? L"Preset RTSS" : T(L"Layout libero"), ToWide(p.name)),
                       ui::col::Good);
              }
            }
            break;
          case IDC_STORE:
            if (UiToProfile() && UiToGlobal()) {
              Profile& p = g.profiles[size_t(g.cur)];
              if (RunGallery(g.wnd, g.inst, g.fontDlg, p)) {
                ProfileToUi();
                OfferUseEverywhere(p);
                SaveAll();
                Status(T(L"Overlay applicato al profilo \"") + ToWide(p.name) + T(L"\" e salvato."), ui::col::Good);
              }
            }
            break;
          case IDC_SENSORS:
            if (UiToProfile() && UiToGlobal()) {
              Profile& p = g.profiles[size_t(g.cur)];
              if (RunSensorBrowser(g.wnd, g.inst, g.fontDlg, p)) {
                Sanitize(p);
                OfferUseEverywhere(p);
                SaveAll();
                Status(TF(L"{} {} nell'overlay di \"{}\": salvato e applicato.", p.sensors.size(),
                                   p.sensors.size() == 1 ? T(L"sensore") : T(L"sensori"), ToWide(p.name)),
                       ui::col::Good);
              }
            }
            break;
          case IDC_NEW: OnNewProfile(); break;
          case IDC_DELETE: OnDeleteProfile(); break;
          case IDC_EXPORT_ONE:
            if (UiToProfile()) Export(ExportProfile(g.profiles[size_t(g.cur)]), ToWide(g.profiles[size_t(g.cur)].name));
            break;
          case IDC_IMPORT_ONE: Import(false); break;
          case IDC_EXPORT_ALL:
            if (UiToProfile() && UiToGlobal()) Export(ExportAll(g.cfg, g.games, g.profiles), L"PerfOverlaySupreme-backup");
            break;
          case IDC_IMPORT_ALL: Import(true); break;
          case IDC_BACKUP_CFG:
            if (UiToGlobal()) Export(ExportConfig(g.cfg, g.games), L"PerfOverlaySupreme-config");
            break;
          case IDC_RESTORE_CFG: Import(true); break;
          case IDC_EDIT_GAMES:
            g.games = LoadGames();  // include quelli appresi dall'overlay nel frattempo
            if (RunGamesEditor(hwnd, g.inst, g.fontDlg, g.games)) {
              const bool saved = SaveGames(g.games);
              NotifyMonitorReload();
              Status(saved ? TF(L"Lista giochi salvata: {} giochi, {} esclusi.",
                                         g.games.known.size() + g.games.learned.size(), g.games.exclude.size())
                           : std::wstring(T(L"Errore nel salvataggio di games.json")),
                     saved ? ui::col::Good : ui::col::Bad);
            }
            break;
          case IDC_OPEN_FOLDER:
            ShellExecuteW(hwnd, L"open", AppDataDir().c_str(), nullptr, nullptr, SW_SHOWNORMAL);
            break;
          case IDC_LAUNCH:
            ShellExecuteW(hwnd, L"open", ExePath().c_str(), nullptr, nullptr, SW_SHOWNORMAL);
            SetTimer(hwnd, kTimerMonitor, 500, nullptr);  // controllo rapido subito dopo l'avvio
            break;
          case IDC_SAVE:
            EndCapture(false);
            g.games = LoadGames();  // include eventuali modifiche manuali a games.json
            OnSave();
            break;
        }
      }
      return 0;
    }
    case kMsgUpdateFound: {
      std::unique_ptr<UpdateInfo> u(reinterpret_cast<UpdateInfo*>(lp));
      g.update = std::move(*u);
      PlaceUpdateButton();
      Status(TF(L"È disponibile la versione v{}: premi \"Aggiorna\" in basso a sinistra.", ToWide(g.update->version)),
             ui::col::Accent);
      return 0;
    }
    case kMsgUpdateDownloaded: {
      std::unique_ptr<std::wstring> err(reinterpret_cast<std::wstring*>(lp));
      OnUpdateDownloaded(wp != 0, *err);
      return 0;
    }
    case WM_DESTROY:
      EndCapture(false);  // riattiva le hotkey del monitor se si chiude durante la cattura
      PostQuitMessage(0);
      return 0;
  }
  return DefWindowProcW(hwnd, msg, wp, lp);
}

HFONT MakeFont(int tenthsOfPoint, int weight) {
  return CreateFontW(-MulDiv(tenthsOfPoint, g.dpi, 720), 0, 0, 0, weight, FALSE, FALSE, FALSE, DEFAULT_CHARSET,
                     OUT_DEFAULT_PRECIS, CLIP_DEFAULT_PRECIS, CLEARTYPE_QUALITY, DEFAULT_PITCH, L"Segoe UI");
}

// Icone della barra laterale: Segoe Fluent Icons (Windows 11), altrimenti Segoe MDL2 Assets (Windows 10).
HFONT MakeIconFont() {
  static const wchar_t* face = [] {
    LOGFONTW lf{};
    lf.lfCharSet = DEFAULT_CHARSET;
    wcscpy_s(lf.lfFaceName, L"Segoe Fluent Icons");
    bool found = false;
    HDC dc = GetDC(nullptr);
    EnumFontFamiliesExW(dc, &lf, [](const LOGFONTW*, const TEXTMETRICW*, DWORD, LPARAM p) -> int {
      *reinterpret_cast<bool*>(p) = true;
      return 0;
    }, reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(&found), 0);
    ReleaseDC(nullptr, dc);
    return found ? L"Segoe Fluent Icons" : L"Segoe MDL2 Assets";
  }();
  return CreateFontW(-S(16), 0, 0, 0, FW_NORMAL, FALSE, FALSE, FALSE, DEFAULT_CHARSET, OUT_DEFAULT_PRECIS,
                     CLIP_DEFAULT_PRECIS, CLEARTYPE_QUALITY, DEFAULT_PITCH, face);
}

// La finestra si può ridimensionare e ingrandire: tutto (controlli, testo, pannelli) scala con lei
// in proporzione al disegno originale da 1000x806 a 96 DPI, centrato se le proporzioni sono diverse.
void Relayout(int clientW, int clientH) {
  if (clientW <= 0 || clientH <= 0) return;
  const double zoom = std::min(double(clientW) / MulDiv(kClientW, g.baseDpi, 96),
                               double(clientH) / MulDiv(kClientH, g.baseDpi, 96));
  const int dpi = std::max(48, int(std::lround(g.baseDpi * zoom)));
  const bool rescale = dpi != g.dpi || !g.font;
  g.dpi = dpi;
  g.ox = std::max(0, (clientW - S(kClientW)) / 2);
  g.oy = std::max(0, (clientH - S(kClientH)) / 2);
  SetPropW(g.wnd, ui::kScaleProp, reinterpret_cast<HANDLE>(static_cast<uintptr_t>(MulDiv(1000, g.dpi, 96))));

  if (rescale) {
    const HFONT old[] = {g.font, g.fontBold, g.fontTitle, g.fontSection, g.fontIcon};
    g.fontIcon = MakeIconFont();
    g.font = MakeFont(95, FW_NORMAL);
    g.fontBold = MakeFont(95, FW_SEMIBOLD);
    g.fontTitle = MakeFont(160, FW_BOLD);
    g.fontSection = MakeFont(100, FW_SEMIBOLD);
    for (const auto& [c, r] : g.layout) SendMessageW(c, WM_SETFONT, reinterpret_cast<WPARAM>(g.font), FALSE);
    for (HFONT f : old)
      if (f) DeleteObject(f);
    if (g.icon) DestroyIcon(g.icon);
    g.icon = static_cast<HICON>(LoadImageW(g.inst, MAKEINTRESOURCEW(IDI_APP), IMAGE_ICON, S(32), S(32), 0));
  }

  HDWP dwp = BeginDeferWindowPos(int(g.layout.size()));
  for (const auto& [c, r] : g.layout)
    if (dwp)
      dwp = DeferWindowPos(dwp, c, nullptr, g.ox + S(r.left), g.oy + S(r.top), S(r.right - r.left),
                           S(r.bottom - r.top), SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE);
  if (dwp) EndDeferWindowPos(dwp);

  if (rescale) {
    for (const auto& [c, r] : g.layout) {
      wchar_t cls[32];
      if (!GetClassNameW(c, cls, 32) || _wcsicmp(cls, L"ComboBox") != 0) continue;
      SendMessageW(c, CB_SETITEMHEIGHT, WPARAM(-1), S(kRowH - 8));
      SendMessageW(c, CB_SETITEMHEIGHT, 0, S(22));
    }
    if (HWND list = Item(IDC_LIST)) SendMessageW(list, LB_SETITEMHEIGHT, 0, S(32));
  }
  // Le combobox modificabili (font) selezionano il testo quando cambiano dimensione: lo deselezioniamo.
  const HWND focus = GetFocus();
  for (const auto& [c, r] : g.layout) {
    wchar_t cls[32];
    if ((GetWindowLongW(c, GWL_STYLE) & 0x3) == CBS_DROPDOWN && GetClassNameW(c, cls, 32) &&
        _wcsicmp(cls, L"ComboBox") == 0 && !IsChild(c, focus))
      SendMessageW(c, CB_SETEDITSEL, 0, MAKELPARAM(-1, 0));
  }
  PlaceUpdateButton();
  RedrawWindow(g.wnd, nullptr, nullptr, RDW_INVALIDATE | RDW_ERASE | RDW_ALLCHILDREN);
}

}  // namespace

int RunSettingsWindow(HINSTANCE inst, int show) {
  g.inst = inst;
  g.dpi = g.baseDpi = int(GetDpiForSystem());
  g.cfg = LoadConfig();
  if (COLORREF accent; ui::ParseHexColor(g.cfg.uiAccent, accent)) ui::SetAccent(accent);
  g.games = LoadGames();
  g.profiles = LoadProfiles();
  g.cur = 0;

  EnableDarkModeForApp();
  ui::Init(inst);
  ui::RegisterColorWheel(inst);
  g.fontIcon = MakeIconFont();
  g.font = MakeFont(95, FW_NORMAL);
  g.fontBold = MakeFont(95, FW_SEMIBOLD);
  g.fontTitle = MakeFont(160, FW_BOLD);
  g.fontSection = MakeFont(100, FW_SEMIBOLD);
  g.fontDlg = MakeFont(95, FW_NORMAL);
  g.brBg = CreateSolidBrush(ui::col::Bg);
  g.brPanel = CreateSolidBrush(ui::col::Panel);
  g.brInput = CreateSolidBrush(ui::col::Input);
  g.brInputOff = CreateSolidBrush(kInputOff);
  g.brSide = CreateSolidBrush(kSidebar);
  g.icon = static_cast<HICON>(LoadImageW(inst, MAKEINTRESOURCEW(IDI_APP), IMAGE_ICON, S(32), S(32), 0));

  WNDCLASSEXW wc{sizeof(wc)};
  wc.lpfnWndProc = WndProc;
  wc.hInstance = inst;
  wc.hCursor = LoadCursorW(nullptr, IDC_ARROW);
  wc.hIcon = LoadIconW(inst, MAKEINTRESOURCEW(IDI_APP));
  wc.hIconSm = static_cast<HICON>(LoadImageW(inst, MAKEINTRESOURCEW(IDI_APP), IMAGE_ICON,
                                             GetSystemMetrics(SM_CXSMICON), GetSystemMetrics(SM_CYSMICON), 0));
  wc.hbrBackground = g.brBg;
  wc.lpszClassName = L"PerfOverlaySupremeSettings";
  RegisterClassExW(&wc);

  // Finestra ridimensionabile e nitida su ogni monitor (DPI per monitor); le finestre secondarie
  // restano come prima, a dimensione fissa.
  constexpr DWORD style = WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW | WS_CLIPCHILDREN;
  const DPI_AWARENESS_CONTEXT prevDpi = SetThreadDpiAwarenessContext(DPI_AWARENESS_CONTEXT_PER_MONITOR_AWARE_V2);
  g.wnd = CreateWindowExW(0, wc.lpszClassName, T(L"PerfOverlay Supreme - Impostazioni"), style, CW_USEDEFAULT, CW_USEDEFAULT,
                          S(kClientW), S(kClientH), nullptr, nullptr, inst, nullptr);
  if (prevDpi) SetThreadDpiAwarenessContext(prevDpi);
  if (!g.wnd) return 1;
  ApplyDarkTitleBar(g.wnd, ui::col::Bg);

  // Dimensione iniziale: quella del disegno ai DPI del monitor, ridotta se lo schermo è più piccolo
  // (es. portatili 1366x768 o scala 150% su 1080p), centrata nell'area di lavoro.
  g.baseDpi = int(GetDpiForWindow(g.wnd));
  MONITORINFO mi{sizeof(mi)};
  GetMonitorInfoW(MonitorFromWindow(g.wnd, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONEAREST), &mi);
  RECT frame{0, 0, 0, 0};
  AdjustWindowRectExForDpi(&frame, style, FALSE, 0, UINT(g.baseDpi));
  const int frameW = frame.right - frame.left, frameH = frame.bottom - frame.top;
  const int workW = mi.rcWork.right - mi.rcWork.left, workH = mi.rcWork.bottom - mi.rcWork.top;
  const int designW = MulDiv(kClientW, g.baseDpi, 96), designH = MulDiv(kClientH, g.baseDpi, 96);
  const double fit = std::min({1.0, (workW * 0.96 - frameW) / designW, (workH * 0.96 - frameH) / designH});
  const int winW = int(designW * fit) + frameW, winH = int(designH * fit) + frameH;
  SetWindowPos(g.wnd, nullptr, mi.rcWork.left + (workW - winW) / 2, mi.rcWork.top + (workH - winH) / 2, winW, winH,
               SWP_NOZORDER | SWP_NOACTIVATE);
  RECT client;
  GetClientRect(g.wnd, &client);
  Relayout(client.right, client.bottom);

  BuildUi();
  RefreshList();
  ProfileToUi();
  GlobalToUi();
  g.syncingAccent = true;  // ruota e codice mostrano il colore in uso, senza riapplicarlo
  SendMessageW(Item(IDC_ACCENT_WHEEL), ui::kMsgWheelSetColor, CurrentAccent(), 0);
  SetUtf8(IDC_ACCENT_HEX, ui::HexColor(CurrentAccent()));
  g.syncingAccent = false;
  ShowPage(0);
  UpdateMonitorState();
  SetTimer(g.wnd, kTimerMonitor, 1500, nullptr);
  StartUpdateCheck();
  ShowWindow(g.wnd, show);

  MSG msg;
  while (GetMessageW(&msg, nullptr, 0, 0) > 0) {
    if (IsDialogMessageW(g.wnd, &msg)) continue;
    TranslateMessage(&msg);
    DispatchMessageW(&msg);
  }
  for (HGDIOBJ o : std::initializer_list<HGDIOBJ>{g.font, g.fontBold, g.fontTitle, g.fontSection, g.fontDlg, g.fontIcon,
                                                  g.brBg, g.brPanel, g.brInput, g.brInputOff, g.brSide})
    DeleteObject(o);
  if (g.icon) DestroyIcon(g.icon);
  ui::Shutdown();
  return 0;
}

}  // namespace po
```

`src/src/settings/theme.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "settings/theme.h"

#include <commctrl.h>
#include <windowsx.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <string>
// gdiplus.h usa min/max non qualificati, disattivati da NOMINMAX.
namespace Gdiplus {
using std::max;
using std::min;
}  // namespace Gdiplus
#include <objidl.h>
#include <gdiplus.h>

#pragma comment(lib, "gdiplus.lib")

namespace po::ui {
namespace {

ULONG_PTR g_gdiplus = 0;
constexpr wchar_t kPropBg[] = L"po.bg";
constexpr wchar_t kPropPrimary[] = L"po.primary";
constexpr wchar_t kPropHover[] = L"po.hover";

Gdiplus::Color Gp(COLORREF c, BYTE a = 255) { return Gdiplus::Color(a, GetRValue(c), GetGValue(c), GetBValue(c)); }

// Scala dei controlli: quella impostata dalla finestra principale (ridimensionabile, vedi kScaleProp)
// oppure i DPI del monitor.
float Scale(HWND h) {
  if (HWND root = GetAncestor(h, GA_ROOT))
    if (const auto v = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(GetPropW(root, kScaleProp))) return float(v) / 1000.0f;
  return GetDpiForWindow(h) / 96.0f;
}

void RoundPath(Gdiplus::GraphicsPath& p, float x, float y, float w, float h, float rad) {
  const float d = std::min(rad * 2, std::min(w, h));
  p.AddArc(x, y, d, d, 180, 90);
  p.AddArc(x + w - d, y, d, d, 270, 90);
  p.AddArc(x + w - d, y + h - d, d, d, 0, 90);
  p.AddArc(x, y + h - d, d, d, 90, 90);
  p.CloseFigure();
}

void TrackLeave(HWND h) {
  TRACKMOUSEEVENT t{sizeof(t), TME_LEAVE, h, 0};
  TrackMouseEvent(&t);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------ pulsante
LRESULT CALLBACK ButtonSubclass(HWND h, UINT msg, WPARAM wp, LPARAM lp, UINT_PTR, DWORD_PTR) {
  switch (msg) {
    case WM_MOUSEMOVE:
      if (!GetPropW(h, kPropHover)) {
        SetPropW(h, kPropHover, reinterpret_cast<HANDLE>(1));
        TrackLeave(h);
        InvalidateRect(h, nullptr, FALSE);
      }
      break;
    case WM_MOUSELEAVE:
      RemovePropW(h, kPropHover);
      InvalidateRect(h, nullptr, FALSE);
      break;
    case WM_ERASEBKGND:
      return 1;  // disegna tutto WM_DRAWITEM: niente sfarfallio
    case WM_NCDESTROY:
      RemovePropW(h, kPropBg);
      RemovePropW(h, kPropPrimary);
      RemovePropW(h, kPropHover);
      RemoveWindowSubclass(h, ButtonSubclass, 0);
      break;
  }
  return DefSubclassProc(h, msg, wp, lp);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------ combobox
void PaintCombo(HWND h, HDC target) {
  RECT rc;
  GetClientRect(h, &rc);
  HDC dc = CreateCompatibleDC(target);
  HBITMAP bmp = CreateCompatibleBitmap(target, rc.right, rc.bottom);
  HGDIOBJ oldBmp = SelectObject(dc, bmp);
  HBRUSH bg = CreateSolidBrush(static_cast<COLORREF>(reinterpret_cast<UINT_PTR>(GetPropW(h, kPropBg))));
  FillRect(dc, &rc, bg);
  DeleteObject(bg);

  const float s = Scale(h);
  COMBOBOXINFO ci{sizeof(ci)};
  GetComboBoxInfo(h, &ci);
  const bool editable = ci.hwndItem && ci.hwndItem != h;
  const bool enabled = IsWindowEnabled(h);
  const bool dropped = SendMessageW(h, CB_GETDROPPEDSTATE, 0, 0) != 0;
  const HWND focus = GetFocus();
  const bool focused = focus == h || (editable && focus == ci.hwndItem);
  const bool hover = GetPropW(h, kPropHover) != nullptr;
  const bool active = enabled && (dropped || focused);
  const COLORREF border = !enabled ? RGB(48, 48, 56) : active ? col::Accent : hover ? col::FieldHover : col::Field;
  FillRound(dc, rc, int(8 * s), enabled ? col::Input : RGB(32, 32, 38), border, active ? 2.0f * s : 1.0f);

  if (!editable) {  // nella combobox modificabile il testo lo disegna il suo campo EDIT
    std::wstring text;
    const int sel = int(SendMessageW(h, CB_GETCURSEL, 0, 0));
    if (sel >= 0) {
      text.resize(size_t(SendMessageW(h, CB_GETLBTEXTLEN, WPARAM(sel), 0)) + 1);
      text.resize(size_t(SendMessageW(h, CB_GETLBTEXT, WPARAM(sel), reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(text.data()))));
    }
    HGDIOBJ oldFont = SelectObject(dc, reinterpret_cast<HFONT>(SendMessageW(h, WM_GETFONT, 0, 0)));
    SetBkMode(dc, TRANSPARENT);
    SetTextColor(dc, enabled ? col::Text : col::Disabled);
    RECT tr{int(9 * s), 0, rc.right - int(26 * s), rc.bottom};
    DrawTextW(dc, text.c_str(), int(text.size()), &tr,
              DT_SINGLELINE | DT_VCENTER | DT_LEFT | DT_END_ELLIPSIS | DT_NOPREFIX);
    SelectObject(dc, oldFont);
  }

  // Freccia: verso il basso, verso l'alto con la tendina aperta.
  Gdiplus::Graphics g(dc);
  g.SetSmoothingMode(Gdiplus::SmoothingModeAntiAlias);
  Gdiplus::Pen pen(Gp(!enabled ? col::Disabled : dropped || hover ? col::Accent : col::Sub), 1.7f * s);
  pen.SetStartCap(Gdiplus::LineCapRound);
  pen.SetEndCap(Gdiplus::LineCapRound);
  pen.SetLineJoin(Gdiplus::LineJoinRound);
  const float cx = rc.right - 15.0f * s, cy = rc.bottom / 2.0f, dx = 4.0f * s, dy = (dropped ? -2.2f : 2.2f) * s;
  const Gdiplus::PointF pts[] = {{cx - dx, cy - dy}, {cx, cy + dy}, {cx + dx, cy - dy}};
  g.DrawLines(&pen, pts, 3);

  BitBlt(target, 0, 0, rc.right, rc.bottom, dc, 0, 0, SRCCOPY);
  SelectObject(dc, oldBmp);
  DeleteObject(bmp);
  DeleteDC(dc);
}

LRESULT CALLBACK ComboSubclass(HWND h, UINT msg, WPARAM wp, LPARAM lp, UINT_PTR, DWORD_PTR) {
  switch (msg) {
    case WM_PAINT: {
      PAINTSTRUCT ps;
      HDC dc = BeginPaint(h, &ps);
      PaintCombo(h, dc);
      EndPaint(h, &ps);
      return 0;
    }
    case WM_PRINTCLIENT:
      PaintCombo(h, reinterpret_cast<HDC>(wp));
      return 0;
    case WM_ERASEBKGND:
      return 1;
    case WM_MOUSEMOVE:
      if (!GetPropW(h, kPropHover)) {
        SetPropW(h, kPropHover, reinterpret_cast<HANDLE>(1));
        TrackLeave(h);
        InvalidateRect(h, nullptr, FALSE);
      }
      break;
    case WM_MOUSELEAVE:
      RemovePropW(h, kPropHover);
      InvalidateRect(h, nullptr, FALSE);
      break;
    // Dopo questi messaggi la combobox nativa si ridisegnerebbe da sola: ridisegno con il tema.
    case WM_SETFOCUS:
    case WM_KILLFOCUS:
    case WM_ENABLE:
    case WM_SETTEXT:
    case WM_KEYDOWN:
    case WM_CHAR:
    case WM_LBUTTONDOWN:
    case WM_LBUTTONUP:
    case WM_MOUSEWHEEL:
    case WM_CAPTURECHANGED:
    case WM_COMMAND:  // notifiche della tendina (chiusura, selezione)
    case CB_SETCURSEL:
    case CB_SHOWDROPDOWN:
    case CB_RESETCONTENT:
    case CB_SELECTSTRING: {
      const LRESULT r = DefSubclassProc(h, msg, wp, lp);
      InvalidateRect(h, nullptr, FALSE);
      return r;
    }
    case WM_NCDESTROY:
      RemovePropW(h, kPropBg);
      RemovePropW(h, kPropHover);
      RemoveWindowSubclass(h, ComboSubclass, 0);
      break;
  }
  return DefSubclassProc(h, msg, wp, lp);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------ checkbox
struct CheckState {
  bool checked = false;
  bool hover = false;
  HFONT font = nullptr;
};

void PaintCheck(HWND h, HDC target) {
  auto* st = reinterpret_cast<CheckState*>(GetWindowLongPtrW(h, GWLP_USERDATA));
  RECT rc;
  GetClientRect(h, &rc);
  HDC dc = CreateCompatibleDC(target);
  HBITMAP bmp = CreateCompatibleBitmap(target, rc.right, rc.bottom);
  HGDIOBJ oldBmp = SelectObject(dc, bmp);

  HBRUSH bg = CreateSolidBrush(col::Panel);
  FillRect(dc, &rc, bg);
  DeleteObject(bg);

  const float s = Scale(h);
  const bool enabled = IsWindowEnabled(h);
  // Focus visibile solo quando serve (navigazione da tastiera), come i controlli di Windows.
  const bool focus = enabled && GetFocus() == h && !(SendMessageW(h, WM_QUERYUISTATE, 0, 0) & UISF_HIDEFOCUS);
  const int box = int(16 * s);
  const RECT br{0, (rc.bottom - box) / 2, box, (rc.bottom - box) / 2 + box};
  if (st->checked) {
    const COLORREF fill = enabled ? col::Accent : Blend(col::Accent, col::Panel, 0.55f);
    // Casella spuntata con il focus: anello chiaro sopra il blu, che altrimenti non cambierebbe.
    FillRound(dc, br, int(4 * s), fill, focus ? col::Text : fill, focus ? 2.0f * s : 1.0f);
    Gdiplus::Graphics g(dc);
    g.SetSmoothingMode(Gdiplus::SmoothingModeAntiAlias);
    Gdiplus::Pen pen(Gp(col::AccentText), 2.2f * s);
    pen.SetStartCap(Gdiplus::LineCapRound);
    pen.SetEndCap(Gdiplus::LineCapRound);
    pen.SetLineJoin(Gdiplus::LineJoinRound);
    const Gdiplus::PointF pts[] = {{br.left + 4.0f * s, br.top + 8.5f * s},
                                   {br.left + 7.0f * s, br.top + 11.5f * s},
                                   {br.left + 12.0f * s, br.top + 5.0f * s}};
    g.DrawLines(&pen, pts, 3);
  } else {
    const COLORREF border = !enabled ? RGB(48, 48, 56) : focus ? col::Accent : st->hover ? col::FieldHover : col::Field;
    FillRound(dc, br, int(4 * s), col::Input, border, focus ? 2.0f * s : 1.0f);
  }

  wchar_t text[256];
  GetWindowTextW(h, text, 256);
  HGDIOBJ oldFont = SelectObject(dc, st->font ? st->font : GetStockObject(DEFAULT_GUI_FONT));
  SetBkMode(dc, TRANSPARENT);
  SetTextColor(dc, enabled ? col::Text : col::Disabled);
  RECT tr{box + int(9 * s), 0, rc.right, rc.bottom};
  DrawTextW(dc, text, -1, &tr, DT_SINGLELINE | DT_VCENTER | DT_LEFT | DT_END_ELLIPSIS | DT_NOPREFIX);
  SelectObject(dc, oldFont);

  BitBlt(target, 0, 0, rc.right, rc.bottom, dc, 0, 0, SRCCOPY);
  SelectObject(dc, oldBmp);
  DeleteObject(bmp);
  DeleteDC(dc);
}

void Toggle(HWND h, CheckState* st) {
  st->checked = !st->checked;
  InvalidateRect(h, nullptr, FALSE);
  SendMessageW(GetParent(h), WM_COMMAND, MAKEWPARAM(GetDlgCtrlID(h), BN_CLICKED), reinterpret_cast<LPARAM>(h));
}

LRESULT CALLBACK CheckProc(HWND h, UINT msg, WPARAM wp, LPARAM lp) {
  auto* st = reinterpret_cast<CheckState*>(GetWindowLongPtrW(h, GWLP_USERDATA));
  switch (msg) {
    case WM_NCCREATE:
      SetWindowLongPtrW(h, GWLP_USERDATA, reinterpret_cast<LONG_PTR>(new CheckState));
      break;
    case WM_NCDESTROY:
      delete st;
      SetWindowLongPtrW(h, GWLP_USERDATA, 0);
      break;
    case WM_PAINT: {
      PAINTSTRUCT ps;
      HDC dc = BeginPaint(h, &ps);
      PaintCheck(h, dc);
      EndPaint(h, &ps);
      return 0;
    }
    case WM_ERASEBKGND:
      return 1;
    case WM_SETFONT:
      st->font = reinterpret_cast<HFONT>(wp);
      if (lp) InvalidateRect(h, nullptr, FALSE);
      return 0;
    case WM_GETFONT:
      return reinterpret_cast<LRESULT>(st->font);
    case WM_SETTEXT:
    case WM_ENABLE:
    case WM_SETFOCUS:
    case WM_KILLFOCUS: {
      const LRESULT r = DefWindowProcW(h, msg, wp, lp);
      InvalidateRect(h, nullptr, FALSE);
      return r;
    }
    case BM_GETCHECK:
      return st->checked ? BST_CHECKED : BST_UNCHECKED;
    case BM_SETCHECK:
      st->checked = wp == BST_CHECKED;
      InvalidateRect(h, nullptr, FALSE);
      return 0;
    case WM_MOUSEMOVE:
      if (!st->hover) {
        st->hover = true;
        TrackLeave(h);
        InvalidateRect(h, nullptr, FALSE);
      }
      return 0;
    case WM_MOUSELEAVE:
      st->hover = false;
      InvalidateRect(h, nullptr, FALSE);
      return 0;
    case WM_LBUTTONDOWN:
      SetFocus(h);
      SetCapture(h);
      return 0;
    case WM_LBUTTONUP: {
      if (GetCapture() == h) ReleaseCapture();
      RECT rc;
      GetClientRect(h, &rc);
      const POINT pt{GET_X_LPARAM(lp), GET_Y_LPARAM(lp)};
      if (PtInRect(&rc, pt)) Toggle(h, st);
      return 0;
    }
    case WM_KEYUP:
      if (wp == VK_SPACE) Toggle(h, st);
      return 0;
    case WM_GETDLGCODE:
      return DLGC_BUTTON;
  }
  return DefWindowProcW(h, msg, wp, lp);
}

}  // namespace

void Init(HINSTANCE inst) {
  Gdiplus::GdiplusStartupInput in;
  Gdiplus::GdiplusStartup(&g_gdiplus, &in, nullptr);

  WNDCLASSEXW wc{sizeof(wc)};
  wc.lpfnWndProc = CheckProc;
  wc.hInstance = inst;
  wc.hCursor = LoadCursorW(nullptr, IDC_HAND);
  wc.lpszClassName = kCheckClass;
  RegisterClassExW(&wc);
}

void Shutdown() {
  if (g_gdiplus) Gdiplus::GdiplusShutdown(g_gdiplus);
  g_gdiplus = 0;
}

bool IsHovered(HWND btn) { return GetPropW(btn, kPropHover) != nullptr; }

COLORREF Blend(COLORREF a, COLORREF b, float t) {
  auto mix = [t](int x, int y) { return BYTE(x + (y - x) * t + 0.5f); };
  return RGB(mix(GetRValue(a), GetRValue(b)), mix(GetGValue(a), GetGValue(b)), mix(GetBValue(a), GetBValue(b)));
}

namespace {
float Luminance(COLORREF c) {
  auto ch = [](BYTE v) {
    const float s = v / 255.0f;
    return s <= 0.03928f ? s / 12.92f : std::pow((s + 0.055f) / 1.055f, 2.4f);
  };
  return 0.2126f * ch(GetRValue(c)) + 0.7152f * ch(GetGValue(c)) + 0.0722f * ch(GetBValue(c));
}
}  // namespace

float Contrast(COLORREF a, COLORREF b) {
  const float la = Luminance(a), lb = Luminance(b);
  return (std::max(la, lb) + 0.05f) / (std::min(la, lb) + 0.05f);
}

void SetAccent(COLORREF accent) {
  // L'accento si usa anche per titoli e bordi di focus: sui pannelli deve leggersi come testo.
  for (int i = 0; i < 20 && Contrast(accent, col::Panel) < 4.5f; ++i) accent = Blend(accent, RGB(255, 255, 255), 0.08f);
  col::Accent = accent;
  col::AccentHover = Blend(accent, RGB(255, 255, 255), 0.25f);
  // Testo sui pulsanti d'accento: quasi nero se l'accento è chiaro, bianco se è scuro.
  const COLORREF dark = Blend(accent, RGB(0, 0, 0), 0.88f);
  col::AccentText = Contrast(dark, accent) >= Contrast(RGB(255, 255, 255), accent) ? dark : RGB(255, 255, 255);
}

bool ParseHexColor(const std::string& s, COLORREF& out) {
  if (s.size() != 7 || s[0] != '#') return false;
  unsigned v = 0;
  for (size_t i = 1; i < 7; ++i) {
    const char c = s[i];
    const int d = c >= '0' && c <= '9' ? c - '0' : c >= 'a' && c <= 'f' ? c - 'a' + 10 : c >= 'A' && c <= 'F' ? c - 'A' + 10 : -1;
    if (d < 0) return false;
    v = v * 16 + unsigned(d);
  }
  out = RGB((v >> 16) & 0xFF, (v >> 8) & 0xFF, v & 0xFF);
  return true;
}

std::string HexColor(COLORREF c) {
  char buf[8];
  snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "#%02X%02X%02X", GetRValue(c), GetGValue(c), GetBValue(c));
  return buf;
}

void FillRound(HDC dc, const RECT& r, int radius, COLORREF fill, COLORREF border, float borderWidth) {
  Gdiplus::Graphics g(dc);
  g.SetSmoothingMode(Gdiplus::SmoothingModeAntiAlias);
  g.SetPixelOffsetMode(Gdiplus::PixelOffsetModeHalf);
  Gdiplus::GraphicsPath p;
  const float inset = borderWidth > 0 ? borderWidth / 2 : 0;
  RoundPath(p, r.left + inset, r.top + inset, float(r.right - r.left) - 2 * inset,
            float(r.bottom - r.top) - 2 * inset, float(radius));
  Gdiplus::SolidBrush b(Gp(fill));
  g.FillPath(&b, &p);
  if (borderWidth > 0) {
    Gdiplus::Pen pen(Gp(border), borderWidth);
    g.DrawPath(&pen, &p);
  }
}

void StrokeRound(HDC dc, const RECT& r, int radius, COLORREF color, float width) {
  Gdiplus::Graphics g(dc);
  g.SetSmoothingMode(Gdiplus::SmoothingModeAntiAlias);
  g.SetPixelOffsetMode(Gdiplus::PixelOffsetModeHalf);
  Gdiplus::GraphicsPath p;
  RoundPath(p, r.left + width / 2, r.top + width / 2, float(r.right - r.left) - width,
            float(r.bottom - r.top) - width, float(radius));
  Gdiplus::Pen pen(Gp(color), width);
  g.DrawPath(&pen, &p);
}

void FillCircle(HDC dc, int cx, int cy, int radius, COLORREF fill, COLORREF border) {
  Gdiplus::Graphics g(dc);
  g.SetSmoothingMode(Gdiplus::SmoothingModeAntiAlias);
  Gdiplus::SolidBrush b(Gp(fill));
  g.FillEllipse(&b, float(cx - radius), float(cy - radius), float(radius * 2), float(radius * 2));
  Gdiplus::Pen pen(Gp(border), 1.0f);
  g.DrawEllipse(&pen, float(cx - radius), float(cy - radius), float(radius * 2), float(radius * 2));
}

void MakeButton(HWND btn, COLORREF background, bool primary) {
  SetPropW(btn, kPropBg, reinterpret_cast<HANDLE>(static_cast<UINT_PTR>(background)));
  if (primary) SetPropW(btn, kPropPrimary, reinterpret_cast<HANDLE>(1));
  SetWindowSubclass(btn, ButtonSubclass, 0, 0);
}

void MakeCombo(HWND combo, COLORREF background) {
  SetPropW(combo, kPropBg, reinterpret_cast<HANDLE>(static_cast<UINT_PTR>(background)));
  SetWindowSubclass(combo, ComboSubclass, 0, 0);
  InvalidateRect(combo, nullptr, FALSE);
}

void DrawButton(const DRAWITEMSTRUCT* dis, const COLORREF* swatch) {
  HWND h = dis->hwndItem;
  const RECT& rc = dis->rcItem;
  const int w = rc.right - rc.left, hgt = rc.bottom - rc.top;
  const float s = Scale(h);

  // Doppio buffer: il pulsante viene ridisegnato a ogni hover.
  HDC dc = CreateCompatibleDC(dis->hDC);
  HBITMAP bmp = CreateCompatibleBitmap(dis->hDC, w, hgt);
  HGDIOBJ oldBmp = SelectObject(dc, bmp);
  const RECT r{0, 0, w, hgt};
  HBRUSH bgBrush = CreateSolidBrush(static_cast<COLORREF>(reinterpret_cast<UINT_PTR>(GetPropW(h, kPropBg))));
  FillRect(dc, &r, bgBrush);
  DeleteObject(bgBrush);

  const bool primary = GetPropW(h, kPropPrimary) != nullptr;
  const bool hover = GetPropW(h, kPropHover) != nullptr;
  const bool disabled = dis->itemState & ODS_DISABLED;
  const bool pressed = dis->itemState & ODS_SELECTED;
  const bool focus = (dis->itemState & ODS_FOCUS) && !(dis->itemState & ODS_NOFOCUSRECT);
  COLORREF fill, border, text;
  if (disabled) {
    fill = RGB(32, 32, 38);
    border = RGB(48, 48, 56);
    text = RGB(96, 96, 106);
  } else if (primary) {
    fill = pressed ? col::Accent : (hover ? col::AccentHover : col::Accent);
    border = focus ? col::Text : fill;  // sul blu il focus è un bordo chiaro
    text = col::AccentText;
  } else {
    fill = pressed ? RGB(36, 36, 42) : (hover ? Blend(col::BtnBg, col::Accent, 0.12f) : col::BtnBg);
    border = focus ? col::Accent : hover ? col::FieldHover : col::BtnBorder;  // hover e focus distinti
    text = col::Text;
  }
  FillRound(dc, r, int(8 * s), fill, border, focus && !disabled ? 2.0f * s : 1.0f);

  wchar_t label[128];
  GetWindowTextW(h, label, 128);
  HGDIOBJ oldFont = SelectObject(dc, reinterpret_cast<HFONT>(SendMessageW(h, WM_GETFONT, 0, 0)));
  SetBkMode(dc, TRANSPARENT);
  SetTextColor(dc, text);
  RECT tr = r;
  UINT fmt = DT_SINGLELINE | DT_VCENTER | DT_NOPREFIX | DT_END_ELLIPSIS;
  if (swatch) {  // pulsante colore: pallino a sinistra + nome
    const int rad = int(7 * s);
    FillCircle(dc, int(14 * s) + rad, hgt / 2, rad, *swatch, Blend(*swatch, RGB(255, 255, 255), 0.35f));
    tr.left = int(14 * s) + rad * 2 + int(10 * s);
    fmt |= DT_LEFT;
  } else {
    fmt |= DT_CENTER;
  }
  DrawTextW(dc, label, -1, &tr, fmt);
  SelectObject(dc, oldFont);

  BitBlt(dis->hDC, rc.left, rc.top, w, hgt, dc, 0, 0, SRCCOPY);
  SelectObject(dc, oldBmp);
  DeleteObject(bmp);
  DeleteDC(dc);
}

}  // namespace po::ui

```

`src/src/settings/theme.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include <windows.h>

#include <string>

// Tema grafico condiviso con le altre app dell'autore (Decky Manager, SteamImporter):
// sfondo quasi nero, pannelli arrotondati, testo chiaro, un colore d'accento per app.
namespace po::ui {

namespace col {
constexpr COLORREF Bg = RGB(15, 15, 17);
constexpr COLORREF Panel = RGB(28, 28, 32);
constexpr COLORREF Line = RGB(52, 52, 60);
constexpr COLORREF Input = RGB(38, 38, 44);
constexpr COLORREF Text = RGB(242, 242, 246);
constexpr COLORREF Sub = RGB(150, 150, 162);
constexpr COLORREF Disabled = RGB(104, 104, 114);
// Colore d'accento scelto dall'utente (Impostazioni › Colore interfaccia): si cambia con SetAccent,
// che ricava anche hover e testo sopra l'accento. Predefinito: blu (Decky arancione, SteamImporter viola).
constexpr COLORREF DefaultAccent = RGB(59, 158, 255);
inline COLORREF Accent = DefaultAccent;
inline COLORREF AccentHover = RGB(107, 182, 255);
inline COLORREF AccentText = RGB(8, 18, 32);
constexpr COLORREF BtnBg = RGB(44, 44, 52);
// Bordi dei controlli (campi, pulsanti, caselle): almeno 3:1 su Panel e Bg, come chiede WCAG 1.4.11.
// Line resta per pannelli e separatori, che sono solo decorativi.
constexpr COLORREF Field = RGB(106, 106, 120);
constexpr COLORREF FieldHover = RGB(150, 150, 166);  // mouse sopra; il focus invece usa Accent e bordo doppio
constexpr COLORREF BtnBorder = Field;
constexpr COLORREF Good = RGB(74, 214, 124);
constexpr COLORREF Bad = RGB(240, 96, 80);
}  // namespace col

inline constexpr wchar_t kCheckClass[] = L"POCheck";
// Proprietà della finestra principale: scala dei controlli × 1000 (1000 = 96 DPI). Se manca si usano i DPI.
inline constexpr wchar_t kScaleProp[] = L"po.scale";

void Init(HINSTANCE inst);  // GDI+ e classi finestra
void Shutdown();

COLORREF Blend(COLORREF a, COLORREF b, float t);  // t=0 → a, t=1 → b

// Imposta il colore d'accento. Un colore troppo scuro viene schiarito quanto basta per restare leggibile
// sullo sfondo (contrasto almeno 4.5:1); il testo sopra l'accento diventa scuro o chiaro a seconda del colore.
void SetAccent(COLORREF accent);
float Contrast(COLORREF a, COLORREF b);  // rapporto di contrasto WCAG (1..21)
// "#RRGGBB" ↔ COLORREF. ParseHexColor restituisce false se la stringa non è valida.
bool ParseHexColor(const std::string& s, COLORREF& out);
std::string HexColor(COLORREF c);
void FillRound(HDC dc, const RECT& r, int radius, COLORREF fill, COLORREF border, float borderWidth = 1.0f);
void StrokeRound(HDC dc, const RECT& r, int radius, COLORREF color, float width);
void FillCircle(HDC dc, int cx, int cy, int radius, COLORREF fill, COLORREF border);

// Pulsante owner-draw (BS_OWNERDRAW): stato hover gestito qui, disegno con DrawButton da WM_DRAWITEM.
void MakeButton(HWND btn, COLORREF background, bool primary);
bool IsHovered(HWND btn);  // il mouse è sopra un pulsante creato con MakeButton
void DrawButton(const DRAWITEMSTRUCT* dis, const COLORREF* swatch = nullptr);

// Combobox con lo stesso aspetto dei campi di testo: cornice arrotondata, bordo d'accento con hover,
// focus o tendina aperta, freccia disegnata. background = colore dietro il controllo.
void MakeCombo(HWND combo, COLORREF background);

}  // namespace po::ui

```

`src/src/settings/updater.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "settings/updater.h"

#include <windows.h>
#include <bcrypt.h>
#include <winhttp.h>

#include <array>
#include <cstdio>
#include <fstream>
#include <vector>

#include <nlohmann/json.hpp>

#include "common/branding.h"
#include "common/i18n.h"
#include "common/log.h"
#include "common/paths.h"
#include "common/util.h"

#pragma comment(lib, "bcrypt.lib")
#pragma comment(lib, "winhttp.lib")

#ifndef PO_VERSION
#define PO_VERSION "0.0.0"
#endif

namespace po {
namespace fs = std::filesystem;

namespace {

struct Handle {
  HINTERNET h = nullptr;
  ~Handle() {
    if (h) WinHttpCloseHandle(h);
  }
};

// GET via WinHTTP (niente cache di Internet Explorer, segue i redirect di GitHub verso il CDN).
// Con out != nullptr la risposta va su file, altrimenti in body.
bool HttpGet(const std::wstring& url, std::string* body, std::ofstream* out) {
  wchar_t host[256] = {}, path[4096] = {}, extra[2048] = {};
  URL_COMPONENTS uc{sizeof(uc)};
  uc.lpszHostName = host;
  uc.dwHostNameLength = DWORD(std::size(host));
  uc.lpszUrlPath = path;
  uc.dwUrlPathLength = DWORD(std::size(path));
  uc.lpszExtraInfo = extra;
  uc.dwExtraInfoLength = DWORD(std::size(extra));
  if (!WinHttpCrackUrl(url.c_str(), 0, 0, &uc) || uc.nScheme != INTERNET_SCHEME_HTTPS) return false;

  Handle s{WinHttpOpen(L"PerfOverlaySupreme-Updater", WINHTTP_ACCESS_TYPE_AUTOMATIC_PROXY, WINHTTP_NO_PROXY_NAME,
                       WINHTTP_NO_PROXY_BYPASS, 0)};
  if (!s.h) return false;
  WinHttpSetTimeouts(s.h, 5000, 10000, 15000, 30000);
  Handle c{WinHttpConnect(s.h, host, uc.nPort, 0)};
  const std::wstring object = std::wstring(path) + extra;
  Handle r{c.h ? WinHttpOpenRequest(c.h, L"GET", object.c_str(), nullptr, WINHTTP_NO_REFERER,
                                    WINHTTP_DEFAULT_ACCEPT_TYPES, WINHTTP_FLAG_SECURE)
               : nullptr};
  if (!r.h) return false;
  const wchar_t* headers = L"Accept: application/vnd.github+json\r\n";
  if (!WinHttpSendRequest(r.h, headers, DWORD(-1), WINHTTP_NO_REQUEST_DATA, 0, 0, 0) ||
      !WinHttpReceiveResponse(r.h, nullptr))
    return false;
  DWORD status = 0, size = sizeof(status);
  WinHttpQueryHeaders(r.h, WINHTTP_QUERY_STATUS_CODE | WINHTTP_QUERY_FLAG_NUMBER, WINHTTP_HEADER_NAME_BY_INDEX,
                      &status, &size, WINHTTP_NO_HEADER_INDEX);
  if (status != 200) {
    LogWarn("Aggiornamento: HTTP {} da {}", status, ToUtf8(url));
    return false;
  }
  std::vector<char> chunk;
  for (DWORD avail = 0; WinHttpQueryDataAvailable(r.h, &avail) && avail > 0;) {
    chunk.resize(avail);
    DWORD read = 0;
    if (!WinHttpReadData(r.h, chunk.data(), avail, &read) || read == 0) return false;
    if (out)
      out->write(chunk.data(), read);
    else
      body->append(chunk.data(), read);
  }
  return out ? bool(*out) : true;
}

std::array<int, 3> ParseVersion(std::string v) {
  if (!v.empty() && (v[0] == 'v' || v[0] == 'V')) v.erase(0, 1);
  std::array<int, 3> n{};
  sscanf_s(v.c_str(), "%d.%d.%d", &n[0], &n[1], &n[2]);
  return n;
}

std::string Sha256(const fs::path& file) {
  BCRYPT_ALG_HANDLE alg = nullptr;
  BCRYPT_HASH_HANDLE hash = nullptr;
  std::string hex;
  if (BCryptOpenAlgorithmProvider(&alg, BCRYPT_SHA256_ALGORITHM, nullptr, 0) == 0 &&
      BCryptCreateHash(alg, &hash, nullptr, 0, nullptr, 0, 0) == 0) {
    std::ifstream f(file, std::ios::binary);
    std::vector<char> buf(1 << 16);
    while (f) {
      f.read(buf.data(), std::streamsize(buf.size()));
      if (f.gcount() > 0) BCryptHashData(hash, reinterpret_cast<PUCHAR>(buf.data()), ULONG(f.gcount()), 0);
    }
    UCHAR digest[32] = {};
    if (BCryptFinishHash(hash, digest, sizeof(digest), 0) == 0)
      for (UCHAR b : digest) hex += std::format("{:02x}", b);
  }
  if (hash) BCryptDestroyHash(hash);
  if (alg) BCryptCloseAlgorithmProvider(alg, 0);
  return hex;
}

// Note di rilascio leggibili: senza le sezioni che GitHub aggiunge da solo.
std::string CleanNotes(std::string s) {
  for (const char* cut : {"## What's Changed", "**Full Changelog**", "## New Contributors"})
    if (const size_t p = s.find(cut); p != std::string::npos) s.erase(p);
  s.erase(std::remove(s.begin(), s.end(), '\r'), s.end());
  s = Trim(s);
  if (s.size() > 700) s = s.substr(0, 700) + "...";
  return s;
}

fs::path OldExePath() { return fs::path(ExePath().wstring() + L".old"); }

}  // namespace

const char* AppVersion() { return PO_VERSION; }

std::optional<UpdateInfo> CheckForUpdate(std::wstring& error) {
  error.clear();
  std::string body;
  if (!HttpGet(ToWide(std::string("https://api.github.com/repos/") + kGitHubRepo + "/releases/latest"), &body,
               nullptr)) {
    error = T(L"GitHub non raggiungibile.");
    return std::nullopt;
  }
  try {
    const auto j = nlohmann::json::parse(body);
    UpdateInfo u;
    u.version = j.value("tag_name", "");
    if (!u.version.empty() && (u.version[0] == 'v' || u.version[0] == 'V')) u.version.erase(0, 1);
    u.notes = CleanNotes(j.value("body", ""));
    u.pageUrl = j.value("html_url", "");
    for (const auto& a : j.value("assets", nlohmann::json::array())) {
      const std::string name = a.value("name", "");
      if (ToLowerAscii(name) != "perfoverlaysupreme.exe") continue;
      u.assetUrl = a.value("browser_download_url", "");
      const std::string digest = a.value("digest", "");
      if (digest.rfind("sha256:", 0) == 0) u.sha256 = ToLowerAscii(digest.substr(7));
    }
    LogInfo("Aggiornamento: ultima release {} (in uso {})", u.version, AppVersion());
    if (u.assetUrl.empty() || ParseVersion(u.version) <= ParseVersion(AppVersion())) return std::nullopt;
    return u;
  } catch (const std::exception& e) {
    LogWarn("Aggiornamento: risposta di GitHub non valida ({})", e.what());
    error = T(L"GitHub non raggiungibile.");
    return std::nullopt;
  }
}

fs::path UpdateDownloadPath() { return fs::path(ExePath().wstring() + L".download"); }

bool DownloadUpdate(const UpdateInfo& u, std::wstring& error) {
  const fs::path dst = UpdateDownloadPath();
  {
    std::ofstream out(dst, std::ios::binary | std::ios::trunc);
    if (!out) {
      error = T(L"Impossibile scrivere nella cartella dell'exe (cartella protetta?): scarica la nuova versione dalla pagina della release.");
      return false;
    }
    if (!HttpGet(ToWide(u.assetUrl), nullptr, &out)) {
      out.close();
      std::error_code ec;
      fs::remove(dst, ec);
      error = T(L"Download non riuscito.");
      return false;
    }
  }
  // Deve essere un eseguibile Windows e, se GitHub ne indica l'impronta, identico a quello della release.
  char mz[2] = {};
  std::ifstream(dst, std::ios::binary).read(mz, 2);
  std::error_code ec;
  const bool exe = mz[0] == 'M' && mz[1] == 'Z' && fs::file_size(dst, ec) > 256 * 1024;
  const std::string hash = Sha256(dst);
  if (!exe || (!u.sha256.empty() && hash != u.sha256)) {
    LogWarn("Aggiornamento: file scaricato non valido (sha256 {} atteso {})", hash, u.sha256);
    fs::remove(dst, ec);
    error = T(L"Il file scaricato non corrisponde a quello della release: aggiornamento annullato.");
    return false;
  }
  LogInfo("Aggiornamento {} scaricato ({} byte, sha256 {}{})", u.version, fs::file_size(dst, ec), hash,
          u.sha256.empty() ? ", impronta non indicata da GitHub" : ", verificato");
  return true;
}

bool InstallUpdate(std::wstring& error) {
  const fs::path exe = ExePath(), old = OldExePath(), dl = UpdateDownloadPath();
  DeleteFileW(old.c_str());  // resto di un aggiornamento precedente
  // L'exe in esecuzione non si può cancellare ma si può rinominare.
  if (!MoveFileExW(exe.c_str(), old.c_str(), MOVEFILE_REPLACE_EXISTING)) {
    error = T(L"Impossibile sostituire l'exe (cartella protetta?): scarica la nuova versione dalla pagina della release.");
    LogWarn("Aggiornamento: rinomina di {} non riuscita (errore {})", ToUtf8(exe.wstring()), GetLastError());
    return false;
  }
  if (!MoveFileExW(dl.c_str(), exe.c_str(), MOVEFILE_REPLACE_EXISTING)) {
    LogWarn("Aggiornamento: sostituzione non riuscita (errore {})", GetLastError());
    MoveFileExW(old.c_str(), exe.c_str(), MOVEFILE_REPLACE_EXISTING);
    error = T(L"Impossibile sostituire l'exe (cartella protetta?): scarica la nuova versione dalla pagina della release.");
    return false;
  }
  LogInfo("Aggiornamento installato in {}", ToUtf8(exe.wstring()));
  return true;
}

void CleanupUpdateFiles() {
  // Il vecchio exe resta occupato finché il vecchio overlay non si chiude: si riprova al prossimo avvio.
  DeleteFileW(OldExePath().c_str());
}

}  // namespace po

```

`src/src/settings/updater.h`:

```h
#pragma once
#include <filesystem>
#include <optional>
#include <string>

// Aggiornamento dall'interno dell'app: legge l'ultima release su GitHub, scarica l'exe, ne verifica
// l'impronta SHA-256 indicata da GitHub e lo mette al posto di quello in uso.
namespace po {

struct UpdateInfo {
  std::string version;   // "1.2.0"
  std::string notes;     // testo della release, senza la parte generata da GitHub
  std::string assetUrl;  // exe da scaricare
  std::string sha256;    // impronta indicata da GitHub (esadecimale minuscolo), vuota se manca
  std::string pageUrl;   // pagina della release
};

const char* AppVersion();  // versione di questo exe ("1.2.0")

// Versione più nuova di questa, se c'è. error resta vuoto se semplicemente non ci sono novità.
std::optional<UpdateInfo> CheckForUpdate(std::wstring& error);

std::filesystem::path UpdateDownloadPath();  // accanto all'exe: <exe>.download
bool DownloadUpdate(const UpdateInfo& update, std::wstring& error);

// Mette l'exe scaricato al posto di quello in uso (che diventa <exe>.old, cancellato al prossimo avvio).
bool InstallUpdate(std::wstring& error);
void CleanupUpdateFiles();

}  // namespace po
```

`src/tools/close-monitor.ps1`:

```ps1
# Chiude PerfOverlaySupreme.exe (anche se gira come amministratore) inviandogli il messaggio di uscita.
Add-Type @"
using System; using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
public static class PoQuit {
  [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern IntPtr FindWindow(string c, string t);
  [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool PostMessage(IntPtr h, int m, IntPtr w, IntPtr l);
}
"@
$h = [PoQuit]::FindWindow("PerfOverlaySupremeMonitor", [NullString]::Value)
if ($h -ne [IntPtr]::Zero) {
  [PoQuit]::PostMessage($h, 0x8004, [IntPtr]::Zero, [IntPtr]::Zero) | Out-Null  # kMsgQuit
  for ($i = 0; $i -lt 20 -and (Get-Process PerfOverlaySupreme -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue); $i++) { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 250 }
}
```
